- **retro**: Produce sprint retrospective and lessons-learned document ([Entelligentsia/forge](https://github.com/Entelligentsia/forge/blob/HEAD/node_modules/%40entelligentsia/forgecli/dist/forge-payload/.base-pack/commands/retro.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)

- **rev-list**: `libra rev-list` 的目标是列出从一个或多个 revision 可达的提交对象。当前实现接受零个或多个 `[SPEC]`（缺省 `HEAD`），支持多 revision union、`^` 排除、`A..B` / `A...B` 范围，并按提交时间倒序打印可达提交哈希；`--count`、`-n`/`--max-count`、`--skip`、`--since`/`--after`、`--until`/`--before`、`--merges`、`--no-merges`、`--min-parents`、`--max-parents`、`--no-min-parents`、`--no-max-parents`、`--first-parent`、`--author`、`--committer`、`--grep`、`-- &lt;PATH&gt;...` path limitation、`- ([libra-tools/libra](https://github.com/libra-tools/libra/blob/HEAD/docs/development/commands/rev-list.md) · ★101)
- **rev-list**: Enumerate the objects reachable from `wants` and not reachable from `not` — the reachability core of git's `git rev-list`. Walks commits (and, with `objects`, their trees and blobs), returning structured data only; no `--pretty`/`--format`/`--date`/`--abbrev`/`--header`/`-z`/`--object-names` — those are all presentation, left to the caller. ([scolladon/tsgit](https://github.com/scolladon/tsgit/blob/HEAD/docs/use/commands/rev-list.md) · ★4)
- **rev-parse**: Parse revision names and print normalized object IDs, symbolic refs, or repository paths. ([libra-tools/libra](https://github.com/libra-tools/libra/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/rev-parse.md) · ★101)
- **rev-parse**: `libra rev-parse` 的目标是解析和规范化 revision 名称、对象 ID 与仓库路径。实现需要支持 `--short`、`--abbrev-ref`、`--show-toplevel` 以及默认 `HEAD` fallback 和错误码，使脚本可以可靠地区分解析失败与使用错误。 ([libra-tools/libra](https://github.com/libra-tools/libra/blob/HEAD/docs/development/commands/rev-parse.md) · ★101)
- **rev-parse**: Resolve a revision argument to the revisions `git rev-parse` prints for it, in git's order. Accepts ref names, oids (full or short), `HEAD`, `@{N}`, `@{date}`, `&lt;ref&gt;~N`, `&lt;ref&gt;^`, `&lt;ref&gt;^N`, the range forms `A..B`, `A...B`, `^&lt;rev&gt;`, and the parent forms `X^@`, `X^!`, `X^-&lt;n&gt;`. ([scolladon/tsgit](https://github.com/scolladon/tsgit/blob/HEAD/docs/use/commands/rev-parse.md) · ★4)

- **revert**: Creates new commits on the current branch that undo the changes introduced by each listed commit. Reverts run **newest first** within a revision range, matching `git revert`. ([gitbutlerapp/grit](https://github.com/gitbutlerapp/grit/blob/HEAD/content/docs/commands/revert.md) · ★598)

- **revert**: `libra revert` 的目标是生成抵消已有提交的反向变更，并保留冲突处理和提交控制的清晰边界。当前实现支持单/多提交、mainline、no-commit、冲突 sequencer、可重复 last-wins 的 `-X/--strategy-option ours|theirs`，以及 `--cleanup=&lt;strip|whitespace|verbatim|scissors|default&gt;`。`-X` 复用 merge 的 hunk resolver，只偏向重叠 region并保留 clean inverse hunk；cleanup 与 favor 都进入 `RevertState`，可跨 conflict→continue。自动提交使用当前 author/committer，并从去签名正文生成 subject。自定义 `--strategy` 仍未实现。 ([libra-tools/libra](https://github.com/libra-tools/libra/blob/HEAD/docs/development/commands/revert.md) · ★101)

- **revert**: `git revert` 命令适用于想要撤销较早提交的某个版本，而不影响之后提交的其他版本。之所以该命令可以实现上述功能，是因为其实质是用一次新的提交来回滚之前较早的某个版本，而不同于 `git reset` 命令直接删除相应提交。因此它不会改写历史，适合用于已推送到远程的公共分支。 ([x2sin1x/x2sin1x.github.io](https://github.com/x2sin1x/x2sin1x.github.io/blob/HEAD/docs/tech-stack/git/commands/revert.md) · ★3)
